`EventEffectFormat.cs`

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Text;

namespace Game.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 효과 결과 줄 문구(설계 89 §5.1) - 한 곳에 모아 대본·테스트·화면이 같은 문구를 쓴다. 음수 부호는 U+2212(−) - TMP 한글 폰트에 없어 기호 대체 폰트가
    /// 처리한다(설계 70).
    /// </summary>
    public static class EventEffectFormat
    {
        private const string Minus = "−";
        public const string NoGoodsLost = "잃은 무역품 없음";

        public static string Gold(int signedAmount) => signedAmount >= 0 ? $"골드 +{signedAmount}" : $"골드 {Minus}{-signedAmount}";
        public static string GoodsGained(string name, int count) => $"{name} ×{count} 획득";
        public static string GoodsLost(string name, int count) => $"{name} ×{count} 잃음";
        public static string GoodsNoneLost(string name) => $"{name} — 잃은 것 없음";

        public static string GoodsLost(IReadOnlyList<GoodsLoss> losses)
        {
            if (losses == null || losses.Count == 0) return NoGoodsLost;
            var sb = new StringBuilder();
            for (var i = 0; i < losses.Count; i++)
            {
                if (i > 0) sb.Append(", ");
                sb.Append(losses[i].Name).Append(" ×").Append(losses[i].Count);
            }
            return sb.Append(" 잃음").ToString();
        }

        public static string Delayed(float seconds) => $"이동 지연 {Seconds(seconds)}초";
        public static string Shortened(float seconds) => $"이동 단축 {Seconds(seconds)}초";

        /// <param name="scope">null = 영구(괄호 없음), "이번 구간", "3일".</param>
        public static string Difficulty(int change, string scope)
        {
            var sign = change >= 0 ? $"+{change}" : $"{Minus}{-change}";
            return scope == null ? $"도로 난이도 {sign}" : $"도로 난이도 {sign} ({scope})";
        }

        public static string Joined(string name) => $"{name} 합류";
        public static string Left(string name) => $"{name} 이탈";

        private static string Seconds(float s) => s.ToString("0.#");
    }
}
```

`EventRoadDifficultyEffects.cs`

```csharp
using System;

namespace Game.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// `<<difficulty 증감 leg|days N|permanent>>`(설계 89 §5.1·§5.2). 이번 구간만 = 현재 구간 실행 값만, 날짜 기간·영구 = 현재 구간의 도시 쌍. 재계산(빈도·남은
    /// 시간·구간 라벨)은 변경을 받은 여정 상태가 한다. 진행 중 구간이 없으면(여정 없음) 실패.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class EventRoadDifficultyEffects
    {
        public const string LegMode = "leg";
        public const string DaysMode = "days";
        public const string PermanentMode = "permanent";

        private readonly ICurrentLegDifficulty leg;
        private readonly ICityPairDifficultyEditor pairs;
        private readonly Func<int> todayTotalDays;

        public EventRoadDifficultyEffects(ICurrentLegDifficulty leg, ICityPairDifficultyEditor pairs, Func<int> todayTotalDays)
        {
            this.leg = leg;
            this.pairs = pairs;
            this.todayTotalDays = todayTotalDays;
        }

        public bool TryApply(int change, string mode, int days, out string resultText)
        {
            resultText = null;
            if (leg == null || !leg.HasLeg) return false;
            switch (mode)
            {
                case LegMode:
                    leg.AdjustCurrentLeg(change);
                    resultText = EventEffectFormat.Difficulty(change, "이번 구간");
                    return true;
                case DaysMode:
                    if (pairs == null || days <= 0 || todayTotalDays == null) return false;
                    pairs.Apply(leg.CurrentPair, leg.CurrentBaseDifficulty, change, todayTotalDays() + days);
                    resultText = EventEffectFormat.Difficulty(change, $"{days}일");
                    return true;
                case PermanentMode:
                    if (pairs == null) return false;
                    pairs.Apply(leg.CurrentPair, leg.CurrentBaseDifficulty, change, null);
                    resultText = EventEffectFormat.Difficulty(change, null);
                    return true;
                default:
                    return false;
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`EventEffectCommands.cs`

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;

namespace Game.Core
{
    /// <summary>이벤트 진행자가 효과 명령에 주는 진행 수단 - 결과 줄 대기·정리 화면·전투 요청.</summary>
    public interface IEventEffectHost
    {
        /// <summary>이벤트가 진행 중인지 - 도주·패배로 끝나면 대기 중인 명령이 빠져나온다.</summary>
        bool IsRunning { get; }
        void PresentResult(string text, Action onDone);
        bool TryShowCargoRecovery(Action onDone);
        /// <summary>전투 요청. 승리로 돌아오면 onVictory - 도주·패배는 진행자가 이벤트를 끝낸다.</summary>
        void RequestBattle(Action onVictory);
    }

    /// <summary>효과 단위 묶음 - 전부 선택적이다(없으면 그 명령만 경고 후 건너뜀).</summary>
    public sealed class EventEffectServices
    {
        public IGoldEventAdjuster Gold;
        public EventCargoGoods Goods;
        public EventRosterEffects Roster;
        public ISessionState Session;
        public ITripClockAdjuster Clock;
        public EventRoadDifficultyEffects Road;
        public Action<string> Warn = _ => { };
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 효과 명령·잠김 조건 함수(설계 89 §5.1·§5.3)의 Yarn 등록과 실행. 명령은 IEnumerator를 돌려 Yarn이 끝날 때까지 기다리게 한다 - 효과 적용 → 결과 줄 →
    /// 클릭을 한 명령 안에서 끝내야 대본 순서와 화면이 어긋나지 않는다. 실행 불가(없는 Id·미연결 서비스·잘못된 인자)는 경고 후 건너뛰고 대본은 계속한다.
    /// 대기는 이벤트가 끝나면(도주·패배) 빠져나온다 - 남은 코루틴이 다음 이벤트를 기다리지 않게.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class EventEffectCommands
    {
        private readonly EventEffectServices services;
        private readonly IEventEffectHost host;

        public EventEffectCommands(EventEffectServices services, IEventEffectHost host)
        {
            this.services = services;
            this.host = host;
        }

        public void Register(IDialogueCommandRegistry registry)
        {
            registry.AddCommand("battle", (Func<IEnumerator>)Battle);
            registry.AddCommand("gold", (Func<int, IEnumerator>)Gold);
            registry.AddCommand("goods_add", (Func<string, int, IEnumerator>)GoodsAdd);
            registry.AddCommand("goods_remove", (Func<string, int, IEnumerator>)GoodsRemove);
            registry.AddCommand("delay", (Func<float, IEnumerator>)Delay);
            registry.AddCommand("difficulty", (Func<int, string, int, IEnumerator>)Difficulty);
            registry.AddCommand("join", (Func<string, IEnumerator>)Join);
            registry.AddCommand("leave", (Func<string, IEnumerator>)Leave);
            registry.AddFunction("gold", (Func<int>)OwnedGold);
            registry.AddFunction("goods", (Func<string, int>)GoodsCount);
            registry.AddFunction("hired", (Func<string, bool>)IsHired);
        }

        public IEnumerator Battle()
        {
            var won = false;
            host.RequestBattle(() => won = true);
            while (!won && host.IsRunning) yield return null;
        }

        public IEnumerator Gold(int amount)
        {
            if (services.Gold == null) { services.Warn("<<gold>>: 골드 서비스가 없다 - 건너뜀"); yield break; }
            if (amount == 0) yield break;
            var actual = amount > 0 ? services.Gold.Gain(amount) : -services.Gold.LoseUpTo(-amount);
            yield return Show(EventEffectFormat.Gold(actual));
        }

        public IEnumerator GoodsAdd(string itemId, int count)
        {
            if (services.Goods == null || count <= 0) { services.Warn($"<<goods_add {itemId} {count}>>: 실행 불가 - 건너뜀"); yield break; }
            if (!services.Goods.TryAdd(itemId, count, out var name, out var staged)) { services.Warn($"<<goods_add>>: 없는 품목 '{itemId}' - 건너뜀"); yield break; }
            if (staged > 0)
            {
                var done = false;
                if (host.TryShowCargoRecovery(() => done = true))
                {
                    while (!done && host.IsRunning) yield return null;
                }
                else
                {
                    services.Warn("<<goods_add>>: 정리 화면이 없어 넘친 무역품이 임시 보관에 남는다(Tools > Game > Build Field Scene).");
                }
            }
            yield return Show(EventEffectFormat.GoodsGained(name, count));
        }

        public IEnumerator GoodsRemove(string target, int count)
        {
            if (services.Goods == null || count <= 0) { services.Warn($"<<goods_remove {target} {count}>>: 실행 불가 - 건너뜀"); yield break; }
            if (target == EventCargoGoods.RandomTarget)
            {
                yield return Show(EventEffectFormat.GoodsLost(services.Goods.RemoveRandom(count)));
                yield break;
            }
            if (!services.Goods.TryRemove(target, count, out var name, out var removed)) { services.Warn($"<<goods_remove>>: 없는 품목 '{target}' - 건너뜀"); yield break; }
            yield return Show(removed > 0 ? EventEffectFormat.GoodsLost(name, removed) : EventEffectFormat.GoodsNoneLost(name));
        }

        public IEnumerator Delay(float seconds)
        {
            if (seconds > 0f)
            {
                if (services.Session == null) { services.Warn("<<delay>>: 상행 시계가 없다 - 건너뜀"); yield break; }
                services.Session.ExtendDuration(seconds);
                yield return Show(EventEffectFormat.Delayed(seconds));
            }
            else if (seconds < 0f)
            {
                if (services.Clock == null) { services.Warn("<<delay>>: 상행 시계 조정이 없다 - 건너뜀"); yield break; }
                yield return Show(EventEffectFormat.Shortened(services.Clock.ShortenDuration(-seconds)));
            }
        }

        public IEnumerator Difficulty(int change, string mode, int days = 0)
        {
            if (services.Road == null || !services.Road.TryApply(change, mode, days, out var text))
            {
                services.Warn($"<<difficulty {change} {mode} {days}>>: 실행 불가(진행 중 구간 없음·잘못된 방식) - 건너뜀");
                yield break;
            }
            yield return Show(text);
        }

        public IEnumerator Join(string characterId)
        {
            if (services.Roster == null) { services.Warn("<<join>>: 고용 명단이 없다 - 건너뜀"); yield break; }
            var result = services.Roster.Join(characterId);
            if (result != RosterEffectResult.Done) { services.Warn($"<<join {characterId}>>: {result} - 건너뜀"); yield break; }
            yield return Show(EventEffectFormat.Joined(services.Roster.NameOf(characterId)));
        }

        public IEnumerator Leave(string characterId)
        {
            if (services.Roster == null) { services.Warn("<<leave>>: 고용 명단이 없다 - 건너뜀"); yield break; }
            var name = services.Roster.NameOf(characterId);
            var result = services.Roster.Leave(characterId);
            if (result != RosterEffectResult.Done) { services.Warn($"<<leave {characterId}>>: {result} - 건너뜀"); yield break; }
            yield return Show(EventEffectFormat.Left(name));
        }

        public int OwnedGold() => services.Gold?.OwnedGold ?? 0;
        public int GoodsCount(string itemId) => services.Goods?.CountOf(itemId) ?? 0;
        public bool IsHired(string characterId) => services.Roster != null && services.Roster.IsHired(characterId);

        private IEnumerator Show(string text)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) || !host.IsRunning) yield break;
            var done = false;
            host.PresentResult(text, () => done = true);
            while (!done && host.IsRunning) yield return null;
        }
    }
}
```

> 확인(제작 중): Yarn이 같은 이름의 명령(`gold`)과 함수(`gold()`)를 함께 등록할 수 있는지 첫 Play에서 콘솔로 확인한다. 충돌하면 함수 이름을 `gold_owned()`로 바꾸고 설계 89 §5.3·대본·Task 13 선언을 같이 고친다.

```csharp
    /// <summary>이벤트 전투 결과(설계 89 §4.1 ⑤). 궤주·사망·포로는 전부 Defeat.</summary>
    public enum EventBattleOutcome { Victory, Flee, Defeat }

    /// <summary>이벤트가 전투를 요청하는 입구(설계 89 §4.2) - 랜덤 전투와 같은 인카운터 흐름으로 들어간다. 적 구성 Id 인자는 향후(§12).</summary>
    public interface IEventBattleRequester
    {
        void RequestEventBattle();
    }

    /// <summary>전투 마무리가 "이벤트" 정지가 남아 있을 때 결과를 넘기는 곳(설계 89 §4.2) - 전투 종료 신호 대신 이걸 부른다.</summary>
    public interface IEventBattleOutcomeSink
    {
        void OnEventBattleFinished(EventBattleOutcome outcome);
    }

    public interface IEventEncounterStatus
    {
        bool IsRunning { get; }
        string CurrentEventId { get; }
        /// <summary>정비창·방향성 지시가 닫히기를 기다리는 이벤트(없으면 null).</summary>
        string PendingEventId { get; }
        IReadOnlyList<EventEntry> QueuedEvents { get; }
    }

    /// <summary>디버그 전용 - 조건·횟수를 무시하고 시작한다. 정비창이 열려 있으면 평소처럼 대기(설계 89 §7).</summary>
    public interface IEventEncounterDebug : IEventEncounterStatus
    {
        bool DebugStart(string eventId);
    }

    /// <summary>Field 로드마다 이번 씬의 이벤트 화면을 진행자에 연결한다 - FieldUIWiring만 쓴다.</summary>
    public interface IEventEncounterViewBinder
    {
        void BindScreens(IEventEncounterScreens screens);
    }
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using NUnit.Framework;
using FakeScreens = Game.Core.Tests.EventPresentationTargetTests.FakeScreens;

namespace Game.Core.Tests
{
    public class EventEncounterDirectorTests
    {
        private sealed class Content : IEncounterContentReader
        {
            public readonly List<EventEntry> List = new();
            public IReadOnlyList<EventEntry> Events => List;
            public IReadOnlyList<DialogueEntry> Dialogues => Array.Empty<DialogueEntry>();
            public IReadOnlyList<PersonEntry> People => Array.Empty<PersonEntry>();
            public bool TryGetEvent(string id, out EventEntry entry) { entry = List.Find(e => e.Id == id); return entry != null; }
            public bool TryGetDialogue(string id, out DialogueEntry entry) { entry = null; return false; }
            public bool TryGetPerson(string id, out PersonEntry entry) { entry = null; return false; }
        }

        private sealed class Runner : IDialogueScriptRunner
        {
            public bool Accept = true;
            public string Node;
            public IDialoguePresentationTarget Target;
            public Action<bool> OnFinished;
            public int StopCount;
            public bool IsRunning => OnFinished != null;
            public bool HasNode(string node) => true;
            public bool TryRun(string node, IDialoguePresentationTarget target, Action<bool> onFinished)
            {
                if (!Accept) return false;
                Node = node; Target = target; OnFinished = onFinished;
                return true;
            }
            public void Stop()
            {
                StopCount++;
                Complete(false);   // 실제 실행기처럼 중단도 완료 콜백을 한 번 부른다
            }
            public void Complete(bool completed)
            {
                var cb = OnFinished;
                OnFinished = null;
                cb?.Invoke(completed);
            }
        }

        private sealed class Halt : ITripHaltControl
        {
            public readonly HashSet<TripHaltReason> Held = new();
            public bool IsHalted => Held.Count > 0;
            public bool IsHeld(TripHaltReason reason) => Held.Contains(reason);
            public event Action HaltStarted { add { } remove { } }
            public event Action HaltEnded { add { } remove { } }
            public void Acquire(TripHaltReason reason) => Held.Add(reason);
            public void Release(TripHaltReason reason) => Held.Remove(reason);
            public void ClearAll() => Held.Clear();
        }

        private sealed class Sink : ITripMilestoneSink
        {
            public readonly List<TripMilestone> Raised = new();
            public void Raise(TripMilestone milestone) => Raised.Add(milestone);
        }

        private sealed class Battle : IEventBattleRequester
        {
            public int Requests;
            public void RequestEventBattle() => Requests++;
        }

        private readonly Content content = new();
        private readonly Runner runner = new();
        private readonly Halt halt = new();
        private readonly Sink sink = new();
        private readonly Battle battle = new();
        private readonly EncounterHistory history = new();
        private FakeScreens screens;

        private EventEncounterDirector Make()
        {
            content.List.Add(new EventEntry { Id = "E1", YarnNode = "E1_Node", Occurrence = EventOccurrence.Fixed });
            var director = new EventEncounterDirector(new EventEncounterSources
            {
                Content = content, History = history, HistoryWriter = history, Runner = runner,
                Halt = halt, Milestones = sink, Battle = battle,
            });
            screens = new FakeScreens();
            director.BindScreens(screens);
            return director;
        }

        [Test]
        public void Check_StartsEvent_HaltsLocksShowsRuns()
        {
            var director = Make();
            director.OnEventCheck();
            Assert.IsTrue(director.IsRunning);
            Assert.AreEqual("E1", director.CurrentEventId);
            Assert.IsTrue(halt.IsHeld(TripHaltReason.Event));
            CollectionAssert.AreEqual(new[] { "Controls:True", "ShowDialogue" }, screens.Calls);
            Assert.AreEqual("E1_Node", runner.Node);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Check_ModalOpen_WaitsThenStartsOnClose()
        {
            var director = Make();
            director.SetModalOpen(true);
            director.OnEventCheck();
            Assert.AreEqual("E1", director.PendingEventId);
            Assert.IsTrue(halt.IsHeld(TripHaltReason.EventPending));
            Assert.IsNull(runner.Node);

            director.SetModalOpen(false);
            Assert.IsNull(runner.Node, "닫힘 신호 처리 중에는 시작하지 않는다(다음 프레임)");
            director.Tick(0.016f);
            Assert.AreEqual("E1_Node", runner.Node);
            Assert.IsFalse(halt.IsHeld(TripHaltReason.EventPending));
            Assert.IsTrue(halt.IsHeld(TripHaltReason.Event));
        }

        [Test]
        public void Finish_Completed_RecordsReleasesSignalsUnlocks()
        {
            var director = Make();
            director.OnEventCheck();
            runner.Complete(true);
            Assert.IsFalse(director.IsRunning);
            Assert.IsTrue(history.HasOccurred("E1"));
            Assert.IsFalse(halt.IsHalted);
            CollectionAssert.AreEqual(new[] { TripMilestone.EventEnded }, sink.Raised);
            CollectionAssert.Contains(screens.Calls, "HideDialogue");
            Assert.AreEqual("Controls:False", screens.Calls[screens.Calls.Count - 1]);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Battle_Victory_KeepsEventHalt_ReopensDialogue_ThenEndsWithBothSignals()
        {
            var director = Make();
            director.OnEventCheck();
            var won = false;
            director.RequestBattle(() => won = true);
            Assert.AreEqual(1, battle.Requests);
            CollectionAssert.Contains(screens.Calls, "HideDialogue");

            screens.Calls.Clear();
            director.OnEventBattleFinished(EventBattleOutcome.Victory);
            Assert.IsTrue(won);
            Assert.IsTrue(director.IsRunning);
            Assert.IsTrue(halt.IsHeld(TripHaltReason.Event));
            CollectionAssert.Contains(screens.Calls, "ShowDialogue");
            CollectionAssert.IsEmpty(sink.Raised);

            runner.Complete(true);
            CollectionAssert.AreEqual(new[] { TripMilestone.EventEnded, TripMilestone.BattleEnded }, sink.Raised);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Battle_Flee_EndsRecorded_ReleasesSignals()
        {
            var director = Make();
            director.OnEventCheck();
            director.RequestBattle(() => { });
            director.OnEventBattleFinished(EventBattleOutcome.Flee);
            Assert.IsFalse(director.IsRunning);
            Assert.IsTrue(history.HasOccurred("E1"), "도주도 끝까지 진행으로 기록");
            Assert.AreEqual(1, runner.StopCount);
            Assert.IsFalse(halt.IsHalted);
            CollectionAssert.AreEqual(new[] { TripMilestone.EventEnded, TripMilestone.BattleEnded }, sink.Raised);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Battle_Defeat_Records_NoReleaseNoSignals()
        {
            var director = Make();
            director.OnEventCheck();
            director.RequestBattle(() => { });
            director.OnEventBattleFinished(EventBattleOutcome.Defeat);
            Assert.IsFalse(director.IsRunning);
            Assert.IsTrue(history.HasOccurred("E1"));
            Assert.IsTrue(halt.IsHeld(TripHaltReason.Event), "상행 종료 신호가 비운다");
            CollectionAssert.IsEmpty(sink.Raised);
        }

        [Test]
        public void RunnerRefuses_ReleasesWithoutRecordOrSignals()
        {
            runner.Accept = false;
            var director = Make();
            director.OnEventCheck();
            Assert.IsFalse(director.IsRunning);
            Assert.IsFalse(halt.IsHalted);
            Assert.IsFalse(history.HasOccurred("E1"));
            CollectionAssert.IsEmpty(sink.Raised);
        }

        [Test]
        public void BindScreensTwice_SubscribesOnce()
        {
            var director = Make();
            director.BindScreens(screens);
            Assert.AreEqual(1, screens.ClickedSubscribers);
        }

        [Test]
        public void NoScreens_CheckDoesNothing()
        {
            var director = Make();
            director.BindScreens(null);
            director.OnEventCheck();
            Assert.IsFalse(director.IsRunning);
            Assert.IsFalse(halt.IsHalted);
        }

        [Test]
        public void TripStarted_ClearsQueue()
        {
            var director = Make();
            director.SetModalOpen(true);
            content.List.Add(new EventEntry { Id = "E4", YarnNode = "E4_Node", Occurrence = EventOccurrence.Fixed });
            director.OnEventCheck();                  // E1 대기, E4 대기열
            Assert.AreEqual(1, director.QueuedEvents.Count);
            director.OnMilestone(TripMilestone.TripStarted);
            Assert.AreEqual(0, director.QueuedEvents.Count);
            Assert.IsNull(director.PendingEventId);
        }

        [Test]
        public void FormationView_OpensViewer()
        {
            var director = Make();
            director.OnEventCheck();
            screens.RaiseFormationView();
            CollectionAssert.Contains(screens.Calls, "Formation");
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 구현** — `EventEncounterDirector.cs`

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace Game.Core
{
    /// <summary>진행자가 쓰는 값·서비스 묶음. 테스트가 필요한 것만 채우도록 기본값을 둔다.</summary>
    public sealed class EventEncounterSources
    {
        public IEncounterContentReader Content;
        public IEncounterHistoryReader History;
        public IEncounterHistoryWriter HistoryWriter;
        public IDialogueScriptRunner Runner;
        public EncounterConditionRegistry Conditions = EncounterConditionRegistry.CreateDefault();
        public Func<EncounterConditionContext> Context = () => default;
        public Func<float> Random01 = () => 0.99f;
        public Func<string, string> NameOf = _ => null;
        public ITripHaltControl Halt;
        public ITripMilestoneSink Milestones;
        public IEventBattleRequester Battle;
        public Action<string> Warn = _ => { };
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 이벤트 진행 규칙(설계 89 §4). Unity를 모르는 순수 C# - FieldManager 하위 껍데기(EventEncounterCoordinator)가 신호·프레임을 넘긴다.
    /// - 판정 → (정비창·방향성 지시가 열려 있으면 "이벤트 대기" 정지 후 닫힌 다음 프레임에) "이벤트" 정지 → 상단 버튼 비활성·열람 모드 → 대화 화면 → 대본.
    ///   닫힘 신호 처리 중에 바로 시작하지 않는 이유: 그 신호 안에서 모달을 다시 열면 같은 신호의 나머지 구독자가 이미 뒤집힌 상태를 받는다.
    /// - 이벤트는 중단되지 않는다(이벤트 중엔 판정·대화 검사가 상행 정지로 멈춘다). 끝까지 진행했을 때만 기록하되, 도주·패배로 흐름이 끝난 것도 "끝"이다.
    /// - 전투: 대화 화면을 닫고 인카운터 흐름에 요청 → 승리면 화면을 다시 열고 대본 계속(이벤트 정지 유지), 도주면 종료 처리, 패배는 기록만 하고 정지·신호는
    ///   기존 패배 처리(상행 종료 신호가 원인을 비운다)에 맡긴다.
    /// 대사 재생 상태는 이벤트마다 새로 만든다 - 이전 이벤트의 일시정지가 남지 않게.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class EventEncounterDirector : IEventEncounterDebug, IEventBattleOutcomeSink, IEventEffectHost
    {
        private static readonly object FormationPauseReason = new();

        private enum Phase { Idle, Pending, Running }

        private sealed class Run
        {
            public EventEntry Entry;
            public DialoguePlaybackController Playback;
            public EventPresentationTarget Target;
            public bool HadBattle;
        }

        private readonly EventEncounterSources sources;
        private readonly SpeakerPresentation speakers;
        private readonly FixedEventQueue queue = new();
        private IEventEncounterScreens screens;
        private Phase phase = Phase.Idle;
        private EventEntry pendingEntry;
        private bool startRequested;
        private bool modalOpen;
        private Run current;
        private Action battleWaiter;

        public EventEncounterDirector(EventEncounterSources sources)
        {
            this.sources = sources;
            speakers = new SpeakerPresentation(sources.NameOf);
        }

        public bool IsRunning => phase == Phase.Running;
        public string CurrentEventId => current?.Entry.Id;
        public string PendingEventId => phase == Phase.Pending ? pendingEntry?.Id : null;
        public IReadOnlyList<EventEntry> QueuedEvents => queue.Snapshot();

        private bool HasScreens => screens != null && screens.IsAvailable;

        public void BindScreens(IEventEncounterScreens newScreens)
        {
            if (screens != null)
            {
                screens.Clicked -= Click;
                screens.FormationViewRequested -= HandleFormationViewRequested;
                screens.Reshown -= HandleReshown;
            }
            screens = newScreens;
            if (screens != null)
            {
                screens.Clicked += Click;
                screens.FormationViewRequested += HandleFormationViewRequested;
                screens.Reshown += HandleReshown;
            }
        }

        public void OnEventCheck()
        {
            if (phase != Phase.Idle || !HasScreens) return;
            var entry = EventEncounterSelector.PickForCheck(queue, sources.Content.Events, sources.History, sources.Conditions, sources.Context(), sources.Random01);
            if (entry != null) Begin(entry);
        }

        public bool DebugStart(string eventId)
        {
            if (phase != Phase.Idle || !HasScreens || !sources.Content.TryGetEvent(eventId, out var entry)) return false;
            Begin(entry);
            return true;
        }

        public void SetModalOpen(bool open)
        {
            modalOpen = open;
            if (!open && phase == Phase.Pending) startRequested = true;
        }

        public void OnMilestone(TripMilestone milestone)
        {
            if (milestone != TripMilestone.TripStarted && milestone != TripMilestone.TripEnded) return;
            queue.Clear();
            if (phase == Phase.Pending)
            {
                phase = Phase.Idle;
                pendingEntry = null;
                startRequested = false;
            }
            else if (phase == Phase.Running && milestone == TripMilestone.TripEnded)
            {
                // 정상 흐름에선 오지 않는다(이벤트 중 상행은 정지) - 방어적으로 조용히 끝낸다. 정지 원인은 조율자가 이미 비웠다.
                var run = current;
                Reset();
                sources.Runner.Stop();
                run?.Playback.Stop();
            }
        }

        public void Tick(float deltaSeconds)
        {
            if (phase == Phase.Pending && startRequested && !modalOpen)
            {
                startRequested = false;
                StartPending();
            }
            current?.Playback.Tick(deltaSeconds);
        }

        public void Click() => current?.Playback.Click();

        // IEventEffectHost
        public void PresentResult(string text, Action onDone)
        {
            if (current == null) { onDone?.Invoke(); return; }
            current.Target.PresentResult(text, onDone);
        }

        public bool TryShowCargoRecovery(Action onDone) => HasScreens && screens.TryShowCargoRecovery(onDone);

        public void RequestBattle(Action onVictory)
        {
            if (current == null) return;
            if (sources.Battle == null)
            {
                sources.Warn("<<battle>>: 전투 입구가 없다 - 승리로 보고 계속한다(Tools > Game > Build Bootstrap Scene).");
                onVictory?.Invoke();
                return;
            }
            current.HadBattle = true;
            battleWaiter = onVictory;
            screens?.HideDialogue();
            sources.Battle.RequestEventBattle();
        }

        // IEventBattleOutcomeSink - 전투 마무리(FinishStep)가 이동 뷰 복귀 뒤에 부른다.
        public void OnEventBattleFinished(EventBattleOutcome outcome)
        {
            var run = current;
            if (run == null) return;
            var waiter = battleWaiter;
            battleWaiter = null;

            switch (outcome)
            {
                case EventBattleOutcome.Victory:
                    if (HasScreens)
                    {
                        screens.SetEventControls(true);
                        screens.ShowDialogue();
                    }
                    waiter?.Invoke();
                    break;
                case EventBattleOutcome.Flee:
                    End(run, record: true, signal: true);
                    sources.Runner.Stop();
                    break;
                default:
                    sources.HistoryWriter?.RecordCompleted(run.Entry.Id);
                    Reset();
                    sources.Runner.Stop();
                    break;
            }
        }

        private void Begin(EventEntry entry)
        {
            pendingEntry = entry;
            phase = Phase.Pending;
            startRequested = false;
            if (modalOpen)
            {
                sources.Halt?.Acquire(TripHaltReason.EventPending);
                return;
            }
            StartPending();
        }

        private void StartPending()
        {
            var entry = pendingEntry;
            pendingEntry = null;
            if (entry == null || !HasScreens)
            {
                phase = Phase.Idle;
                sources.Halt?.Release(TripHaltReason.EventPending);
                return;
            }

            // 이벤트 정지를 먼저 걸고 대기 정지를 푼다 - 반대면 그 사이 상행이 한 번 재개된다.
            sources.Halt?.Acquire(TripHaltReason.Event);
            sources.Halt?.Release(TripHaltReason.EventPending);

            var playback = new DialoguePlaybackController(new StagedDialogueView(screens.DialogueStage, speakers), DialoguePlaybackOptions.Event);
            var run = new Run { Entry = entry, Playback = playback, Target = new EventPresentationTarget(playback, screens, speakers) };
            current = run;
            phase = Phase.Running;
            screens.SetEventControls(true);
            screens.ShowDialogue();

            if (!sources.Runner.TryRun(entry.YarnNode, run.Target, completed => HandleScriptFinished(run, completed)))
            {
                sources.Warn($"이벤트 '{entry.Id}': Yarn 노드 '{entry.YarnNode}'를 실행하지 못했다.");
                End(run, record: false, signal: false);
            }
        }

        private void HandleScriptFinished(Run run, bool completed)
        {
            if (current != run) return;
            End(run, record: completed, signal: true);
        }

        private void End(Run run, bool record, bool signal)
        {
            if (current != run) return;
            Reset();
            if (record) sources.HistoryWriter?.RecordCompleted(run.Entry.Id);
            if (HasScreens)
            {
                screens.HideChoice();
                screens.HideDialogue();
                screens.SetEventControls(false);
            }
            sources.Halt?.Release(TripHaltReason.Event);
            if (!signal) return;
            sources.Milestones?.Raise(TripMilestone.EventEnded);
            if (run.HadBattle) sources.Milestones?.Raise(TripMilestone.BattleEnded);
        }

        private void Reset()
        {
            current = null;
            phase = Phase.Idle;
            battleWaiter = null;
        }

        private void HandleFormationViewRequested()
        {
            if (current == null || !HasScreens) return;
            current.Playback.SetPaused(FormationPauseReason, true);
            screens.OpenFormationViewer();
        }

        private void HandleReshown() => current?.Playback.SetPaused(FormationPauseReason, false);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트 작성** — `FinishStepTripSignalTests.cs`의 `RecordingHalt`를 원인 집합을 갖도록 바꾸고 테스트를 추가한다.

```csharp
        private sealed class RecordingHalt : ITripHaltControl
        {
            public readonly List<string> Calls = new();
            public readonly HashSet<TripHaltReason> Held = new();
            public bool IsHalted => Held.Count > 0;
            public bool IsHeld(TripHaltReason reason) => Held.Contains(reason);
            public event Action HaltStarted { add { } remove { } }
            public event Action HaltEnded { add { } remove { } }
            public void Acquire(TripHaltReason reason) { Calls.Add($"Acquire:{reason}"); Held.Add(reason); }
            public void Release(TripHaltReason reason) { Calls.Add($"Release:{reason}"); Held.Remove(reason); }
            public void ClearAll() { Calls.Add("ClearAll"); Held.Clear(); }
        }

        private sealed class RecordingEventSink : IEventBattleOutcomeSink
        {
            public readonly List<EventBattleOutcome> Outcomes = new();
            public void OnEventBattleFinished(EventBattleOutcome outcome) => Outcomes.Add(outcome);
        }

        [Test]
        public void EventBattle_Victory_KeepsHalt_NoBattleEnded()
        {
            var halt = new RecordingHalt();
            halt.Held.Add(TripHaltReason.Event);
            halt.Held.Add(TripHaltReason.Battle);
            var sink = new RecordingSink();
            var events = new RecordingEventSink();
            FinishStep.ResumeTrip(new FieldTripSignals(halt, sink, events), () => Assert.Fail("직접 재개 금지"), EventBattleOutcome.Victory);
            CollectionAssert.AreEqual(new[] { "Release:Battle" }, halt.Calls);
            Assert.IsTrue(halt.IsHeld(TripHaltReason.Event), "이벤트 정지는 남는다");
            CollectionAssert.IsEmpty(sink.Raised, "전투 종료 신호는 이벤트가 끝날 때");
            CollectionAssert.AreEqual(new[] { EventBattleOutcome.Victory }, events.Outcomes);
        }

        [Test]
        public void EventBattle_Flee_PassesFlee()
        {
            var halt = new RecordingHalt();
            halt.Held.Add(TripHaltReason.Event);
            var events = new RecordingEventSink();
            FinishStep.ResumeTrip(new FieldTripSignals(halt, new RecordingSink(), events), () => { }, EventBattleOutcome.Flee);
            CollectionAssert.AreEqual(new[] { EventBattleOutcome.Flee }, events.Outcomes);
        }

        [Test]
        public void IsInEvent_OnlyWhenEventHeldAndSinkPresent()
        {
            var halt = new RecordingHalt();
            Assert.IsFalse(FinishStep.IsInEvent(new FieldTripSignals(halt, null, new RecordingEventSink())));
            halt.Held.Add(TripHaltReason.Event);
            Assert.IsTrue(FinishStep.IsInEvent(new FieldTripSignals(halt, null, new RecordingEventSink())));
            Assert.IsFalse(FinishStep.IsInEvent(new FieldTripSignals(halt, null, null)));
        }
```

(기존 두 테스트 `ContinueTrip_ReleasesBattle_ThenRaisesBattleEnded`·`ContinueTrip_WithoutHalt_UsesLegacyResume`는 그대로 통과해야 한다 — 이벤트 정지가 없으면 예전 동작.)

`FieldTripSignals.cs` — 세 번째 계약 추가:

```csharp
        public IEventBattleOutcomeSink EventBattle { get; }

        public FieldTripSignals(ITripHaltControl halt, ITripMilestoneSink milestones, IEventBattleOutcomeSink eventBattle = null)
        {
            Halt = halt;
            Milestones = milestones;
            EventBattle = eventBattle;
        }

        public static FieldTripSignals Resolve(IDependencyResolver registrar)
        {
            registrar.TryResolve<ITripHaltControl>(out var halt);
            registrar.TryResolve<ITripMilestoneSink>(out var milestones);
            registrar.TryResolve<IEventBattleOutcomeSink>(out var eventBattle);
            return new FieldTripSignals(halt, milestones, eventBattle);
        }
```

(요약 주석에 "이벤트 전투 결과 전달(설계 89 §4.2)" 추가.)

`FinishStep.cs` — `Run`의 상행 이어가기 분기와 패배 분기, `ResumeTrip`:

```csharp
            if (context.ContinuesTrip)
            {
```

```csharp
        /// <summary>
        /// 상행을 이어갈 때의 재개(설계 87 §6.2·§6.4, 89 §4.2). 정지 조율자가 있으면 "전투" 원인만 푼다. "이벤트" 정지가 남아 있으면 전투 종료 신호를 보내지 않고
        /// 결과를 이벤트 진행자에 넘긴다 - 신호는 이벤트가 끝날 때 진행자가 보낸다. 조율자가 없으면(Bootstrap 미재빌드) 예전처럼 직접 재개한다.
        /// </summary>
        internal static void ResumeTrip(FieldTripSignals tripSignals, Action legacyResume, EventBattleOutcome outcome = EventBattleOutcome.Victory)
        {
            if (tripSignals?.Halt != null)
            {
                var inEvent = IsInEvent(tripSignals);
                tripSignals.Halt.Release(TripHaltReason.Battle);
                if (inEvent)
                {
                    tripSignals.EventBattle.OnEventBattleFinished(outcome);
                    return;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                legacyResume();
            }
            tripSignals?.Milestones?.Raise(TripMilestone.BattleEnded);
        }

        internal static bool IsInEvent(FieldTripSignals tripSignals)
            => tripSignals?.Halt != null && tripSignals.EventBattle != null && tripSignals.Halt.IsHeld(TripHaltReason.Event);
```

`EncounterManager.cs` — 선언에 `IEventBattleRequester` 추가, `RegisterSelf`에 `registrar.Register<IEventBattleRequester>(this);`:

```csharp
        /// <summary>
        /// 이벤트 전투 입구(설계 89 §4.2) - 확률 판정 없이 랜덤 전투와 같은 흐름(경고 → 전투 → 결과 정리)으로 들어간다. 적 구성도 같은 규칙(도로 난이도).
        /// 이벤트 정지가 걸린 상태라 "전투" 원인은 추가로 걸리고, 마무리에서 그것만 풀린다.
        /// </summary>
        public void RequestEventBattle() => TriggerEncounter();
```

```csharp
    /// <summary>
```

```csharp
        private IFieldFormationEditPolicy viewOnlyPolicy;
        private IFieldFormationEditPolicy basePolicy;   // 정리 모드가 아닐 때의 정책 - 평소 또는 열람

        public void SetViewOnly(bool viewOnly)
        {
            basePolicy = viewOnly ? viewOnlyPolicy : timedPolicy;
            if (!IsRepairing) currentPolicy = basePolicy;
            gridEditor?.RequestRefresh();
        }
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1~12 전부
- Produces: DI 등록 `IEventEncounterViewBinder`·`IEventEncounterDebug`·`IEventBattleOutcomeSink`(FieldManager가 대신 등록), Yarn 컴파일 선언 `gold() → number`, `goods(string) → number`, `hired(string) → bool`

- [ ] **Step 1: 판정 문맥 공용화** — `EncounterContextSource.cs` (TripDialogueCoordinator의 `BuildContext`·`RegionOf`를 옮긴다 - 이벤트 진행자도 같은 문맥을 써야 조건 판정이 어긋나지 않는다)

```csharp
using UnityEngine;

namespace Game.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 이벤트·대화 조건 판정 문맥(설계 87 §5.1)을 판정 순간 값으로 만든다. 대화 진행자(88)와 이벤트 진행자(89)가 같은 문맥을 써야 같은 조건이 같게
    /// 판정되므로 한 곳에 둔다. 의존성은 전부 선택적 - 없으면 기본값.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class EncounterContextSource
    {
        private readonly IDateReader date;
        private readonly ITripItinerary itinerary;
        private readonly IWorldMapReader worldMap;
        private readonly ISessionState session;
        private readonly IEncounterHistoryReader history;

        public EncounterContextSource(IDependencyResolver registrar)
        {
            registrar.TryResolve(out date);
            registrar.TryResolve(out itinerary);
            registrar.TryResolve(out worldMap);
            registrar.TryResolve(out session);
            registrar.TryResolve(out history);
        }

        public EncounterConditionContext Build()
        {
            var hasLeg = itinerary != null && itinerary.HasItinerary;
            var today = date != null ? date.Today : default;
            return EncounterConditionContextFactory.Create(today, hasLeg, hasLeg ? itinerary.CurrentLeg : default, RegionOf, session != null ? session.Progress : 0f, history);
        }

        private int RegionOf(int cityId) => worldMap != null && worldMap.TryGetCity(cityId, out var city) ? city.RegionId : -1;
    }
}
```

`TripDialogueCoordinator.cs` — `date`·`itinerary`·`worldMap` 필드와 `BuildContext`·`RegionOf`를 지우고 `var context = new EncounterContextSource(registrar);` 후 `Context = context.Build`. (`session`·`history`는 다른 용도로 쓰므로 유지.) `ut.sh TripDialogueDirectorTests` 회귀.

- [ ] **Step 2: 진행자 껍데기** — `EventEncounterCoordinator.cs`

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Game.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// FieldManager 하위 컴포넌트 - 이벤트 진행 규칙(EventEncounterDirector)을 Bootstrap 수명에 붙인다(설계 89 §3). 의존성 조회·효과 명령 등록·신호 구독·
    /// 프레임 진행만 맡는다. 화면은 Field 로드마다 BindScreens로 바뀌고, 씬이 언로드되면 다음 프레임에 연결을 끊는다. 대사 실행기·콘텐츠·이력 중 하나라도
    /// 없으면(Bootstrap 미재빌드) 이벤트 없이 상행만 진행한다. 효과 서비스는 전부 선택적 - 없는 효과 명령만 경고 후 건너뛴다.
    /// Yarn 명령은 실행기 하나에 한 번만 등록한다(같은 이름 재등록 오류 방지).
    /// </summary>
    public class EventEncounterCoordinator : MonoBehaviour, IEventEncounterViewBinder, IEventEncounterDebug, IEventBattleOutcomeSink
    {
        private static readonly IReadOnlyList<EventEntry> NoEvents = new EventEntry[0];

        private EventEncounterDirector director;
        private ITripCheckSignal checks;
        private ITripMilestoneSource milestones;
        private IUIVisibilitySignal visibility;
        private IEventEncounterScreens boundScreens;
        private IDialogueCommandRegistry registeredOn;

        public bool IsRunning => director != null && director.IsRunning;
        public string CurrentEventId => director?.CurrentEventId;
        public string PendingEventId => director?.PendingEventId;
        public IReadOnlyList<EventEntry> QueuedEvents => director != null ? director.QueuedEvents : NoEvents;

        public void Configure(IDependencyResolver registrar, ITripCheckSignal checks, ITripMilestoneSource milestones, ITripHaltControl halt, ITripMilestoneSink milestoneSink)
        {
            Unsubscribe();
            director = null;

            registrar.TryResolve(out IEncounterContentReader content);
            registrar.TryResolve(out IEncounterHistoryReader history);
            registrar.TryResolve(out IEncounterHistoryWriter historyWriter);
            registrar.TryResolve(out IDialogueScriptRunner runner);
            if (content == null || history == null || historyWriter == null || runner == null)
            {
                Debug.LogWarning($"{nameof(EventEncounterCoordinator)}: 콘텐츠·이력·대사 실행기 중 없는 것이 있어 이벤트가 발생하지 않는다(Tools > Game > Build Bootstrap Scene).");
                return;
            }

            registrar.TryResolve(out ICharacterCatalogReader catalog);
            registrar.TryResolve(out IEventBattleRequester battle);
            registrar.TryResolve(out IUIManager uiManager);
            var context = new EncounterContextSource(registrar);

            this.checks = checks;
            this.milestones = milestones;
            visibility = uiManager;

            director = new EventEncounterDirector(new EventEncounterSources
            {
                Content = content,
                History = history,
                HistoryWriter = historyWriter,
                Runner = runner,
                Context = context.Build,
                Random01 = () => Random.value,
                NameOf = id => NameOf(id, catalog, content),
                Halt = halt,
                Milestones = milestoneSink,
                Battle = battle,
                Warn = message => Debug.LogWarning(message),
            });

            if (registrar.TryResolve(out IDialogueCommandRegistry commands) && commands != registeredOn)
            {
                new EventEffectCommands(BuildServices(registrar, catalog), new HostRelay(this)).Register(commands);
                registeredOn = commands;
            }

            if (checks != null) checks.EventCheck += director.OnEventCheck;
            if (milestones != null) milestones.Raised += director.OnMilestone;
            if (visibility != null)
            {
                visibility.ModalPopupOpenChanged += director.SetModalOpen;
                director.SetModalOpen(visibility.IsModalPopupOpen);
            }
            director.BindScreens(boundScreens);
        }

        public void BindScreens(IEventEncounterScreens screens)
        {
            boundScreens = screens;
            director?.BindScreens(screens);
        }

        public bool DebugStart(string eventId) => director != null && director.DebugStart(eventId);

        public void OnEventBattleFinished(EventBattleOutcome outcome) => director?.OnEventBattleFinished(outcome);

        private void Update()
        {
            if (director == null) return;
            if (boundScreens != null && !boundScreens.IsAvailable)
            {
                BindScreens(null);
                return;
            }
            director.Tick(Time.deltaTime);
        }

        private EventEffectServices BuildServices(IDependencyResolver registrar, ICharacterCatalogReader catalog)
        {
            registrar.TryResolve(out IGoldEventAdjuster gold);
            registrar.TryResolve(out ITradeGoodsInventoryRepository inventory);
            registrar.TryResolve(out ITradeGoodsCargoSettlement settlement);
            registrar.TryResolve(out IHiredCharacterRoster roster);
            registrar.TryResolve(out IHiredCharacterRemover remover);
            registrar.TryResolve(out IFormationRepository formation);
            registrar.TryResolve(out IFieldFormationActivityRepository activities);
            registrar.TryResolve(out ISessionState session);
            registrar.TryResolve(out ITripClockAdjuster clock);
            registrar.TryResolve(out ICurrentLegDifficulty leg);
            registrar.TryResolve(out ICityPairDifficultyEditor pairs);
            registrar.TryResolve(out IDateReader date);

            return new EventEffectServices
            {
                Gold = gold,
                Goods = inventory != null && settlement != null
                    ? new EventCargoGoods(inventory, settlement, id => gold != null && gold.IsGoldItem(id), n => Random.Range(0, n))
                    : null,
                Roster = roster != null
                    ? new EventRosterEffects(catalog, roster, remover, formation, id => activities != null && activities.IsUnitBusy(id), id => activities?.Cancel(id))
                    : null,
                Session = session,
                Clock = clock,
                Road = leg != null ? new EventRoadDifficultyEffects(leg, pairs, () => date != null ? date.Today.TotalDays : 0) : null,
                Warn = message => Debug.LogWarning(message),
            };
        }

        private static string NameOf(string id, ICharacterCatalogReader catalog, IEncounterContentReader content)
        {
            if (catalog != null && catalog.TryGet(id, out var profile)) return profile.Name;
            return content.TryGetPerson(id, out var person) ? person.Name : null;
        }

        private void Unsubscribe()
        {
            if (director == null) return;
            if (checks != null) checks.EventCheck -= director.OnEventCheck;
            if (milestones != null) milestones.Raised -= director.OnMilestone;
            if (visibility != null) visibility.ModalPopupOpenChanged -= director.SetModalOpen;
        }

        private void OnDestroy() => Unsubscribe();

        // 명령은 실행기에 한 번만 등록하지만 진행자는 Configure마다 새로 만든다 - 명령이 항상 현재 진행자를 보게 한 단계 거친다.
        private sealed class HostRelay : IEventEffectHost
        {
            private readonly EventEncounterCoordinator owner;
            public HostRelay(EventEncounterCoordinator owner) => this.owner = owner;
            private IEventEffectHost Host => owner.director;
            public bool IsRunning => Host != null && Host.IsRunning;
            public void PresentResult(string text, System.Action onDone) { if (Host != null) Host.PresentResult(text, onDone); else onDone?.Invoke(); }
            public bool TryShowCargoRecovery(System.Action onDone) => Host != null && Host.TryShowCargoRecovery(onDone);
            public void RequestBattle(System.Action onVictory) { if (Host != null) Host.RequestBattle(onVictory); }
        }
    }
}
```

> 주의: `EventEffectServices`도 첫 Configure 때 만든 것을 명령이 계속 쓴다. 서비스 대상(저장소들)은 Bootstrap 상주라 Configure를 다시 해도 같은 인스턴스다.

- [ ] **Step 4: Yarn 컴파일 선언** — `Narrative.Yarn/EventYarnFunctionDeclarations.cs`

```csharp
#nullable enable
using System;
using UnityEngine;
using Yarn.Unity;

namespace Game.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 잠김 조건 함수(설계 89 §5.3)의 Yarn 컴파일러 선언. Yarn은 대본을 컴파일할 때 함수 이름·타입을 알아야 한다(모르면 컴파일 오류) - 실행 시 구현은
    /// EventEffectCommands가 실행기에 등록하지만, 그 등록은 Play 중에만 있으므로 컴파일용 선언을 여기서 따로 낸다. 이름·타입은 EventEffectCommands.Register와
    /// 같아야 한다.
    /// </summary>
    public static class EventYarnFunctionDeclarations
    {
#if UNITY_EDITOR
        [UnityEditor.InitializeOnLoadMethod]
#endif
        [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.BeforeSceneLoad)]
        private static void AddRegistration() => Actions.AddRegistrationMethod(Register);

        private static void Register(IActionRegistration target, RegistrationType registrationType)
        {
            if (registrationType != RegistrationType.Compilation) return;
            target.RegisterFunctionDeclaration("gold", typeof(int), Type.EmptyTypes);
            target.RegisterFunctionDeclaration("goods", typeof(int), new[] { typeof(string) });
            target.RegisterFunctionDeclaration("hired", typeof(bool), new[] { typeof(string) });
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;

{
    /// <summary>
    /// 화면 루트를 켜고 끄기만 하는 IUIPanel(이벤트 대화·선택 화면, 설계 89 §6.3). 모달 묶음이 정비창·회수 적재를 열면 숨겼다가 닫히면 다시 여는데, 그
    /// "다시 열림"을 진행자가 알아야 대사 일시정지를 풀 수 있어 Opened를 낸다. plain C# - FieldUIWiring이 Field 로드마다 새로 만든다.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class ViewRootPanel : IUIPanel
    {
        private readonly GameObject root;

        public ViewRootPanel(string panelId, GameObject root)
        {
            PanelId = panelId;
            this.root = root;
            root.SetActive(false);
        }

        public string PanelId { get; }
        public event Action Opened;

        public void Open()
        {
            root.SetActive(true);
            Opened?.Invoke();
        }

        // Close()는 표시/숨김만 한다. 이벤트를 끝내 화면을 닫는 것은 진행자가 화면 묶음(FieldEventScreens)을 거쳐 UIManager.Close(PanelId)로 한다.
        public void Close() => root.SetActive(false);
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Game.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 이벤트 대화 화면(설계 89 §6.1) - 전체화면 반투명 배경 위 말풍선(일러1·2 사이)·좌우 스탠딩. 표시 전용 - 자리·밝기·이름은 StagedDialogueView가 정한다.
    /// 꼬리는 밝게 표시된(말하는) 자리를 가리키고, 서술·결과 줄(양쪽 어두움)은 숨긴다. 선택지는 선택 화면이 맡아 여기서는 그리지 않는다.
    /// 배경 전체가 클릭 받기라 화면 아무 곳이나 누르면 넘어간다. 루트 켜기·끄기는 패널(ViewRootPanel)이 한다.
    /// </summary>
    public class FieldEventDialogueView : MonoBehaviour, IDialogueStageView
    {
        private const float DimFactor = 0.45f;

        [SerializeField] private GameObject body;
        [SerializeField] private FieldDialogueClickCatcher clickCatcher;
        [SerializeField] private TMP_Text speakerNameText;
        [SerializeField] private TMP_Text lineText;
        [SerializeField] private GameObject continueMarker;
        [SerializeField] private GameObject leftTail;
        [SerializeField] private GameObject rightTail;
        [SerializeField] private GameObject leftRoot;
        [SerializeField] private Image leftFigure;
        [SerializeField] private TMP_Text leftName;
        [SerializeField] private GameObject rightRoot;
        [SerializeField] private Image rightFigure;
        [SerializeField] private TMP_Text rightName;
        [SerializeField] private Button formationButton;

        private bool leftActive;
        private bool rightActive;

        public event Action Clicked;
        public event Action FormationRequested;

        private void OnEnable()
        {
            if (clickCatcher != null) clickCatcher.Clicked += RaiseClicked;
            if (formationButton != null) formationButton.onClick.AddListener(RaiseFormation);
        }

        private void OnDisable()
        {
            if (clickCatcher != null) clickCatcher.Clicked -= RaiseClicked;
            if (formationButton != null) formationButton.onClick.RemoveListener(RaiseFormation);
        }

        private void RaiseClicked() => Clicked?.Invoke();
        private void RaiseFormation() => FormationRequested?.Invoke();

        public void SetVisible(bool visible) => body.SetActive(visible);

        public void SetBubble(string speakerName, string text)
        {
            speakerNameText.text = speakerName;
            lineText.text = text;
            lineText.maxVisibleCharacters = 0;
        }

        public void SetVisibleCharacters(int count) => lineText.maxVisibleCharacters = count;
        public void SetContinueMarker(bool shown) => continueMarker.SetActive(shown);

        public void SetStanding(DialogueSide side, StandingState state)
        {
            var root = side == DialogueSide.Left ? leftRoot : rightRoot;
            var figure = side == DialogueSide.Left ? leftFigure : rightFigure;
            var nameText = side == DialogueSide.Left ? leftName : rightName;
            var active = state.Visible && state.Highlighted;
            if (side == DialogueSide.Left) leftActive = active; else rightActive = active;
            leftTail.SetActive(leftActive);
            rightTail.SetActive(rightActive);

            root.SetActive(state.Visible);
            if (!state.Visible) return;
            var color = state.Highlighted ? state.Color : state.Color * DimFactor;
            color.a = 1f;
            figure.color = color;
            nameText.text = state.Name;
        }

        // 선택지는 선택 화면(FieldEventChoiceView)이 그린다 - EventPresentationTarget이 이 화면으로 보내지 않는다.
        public void ShowOptions(IReadOnlyList<DialogueOptionData> options, Action<int> onChosen) => Debug.LogWarning($"{nameof(FieldEventDialogueView)}: 선택지는 선택 화면이 그린다.");
        public void SetOptionTimeRemaining(float fraction01) { }
        public void HideOptions() { }
    }
}
```

`FieldEventChoiceView.cs`

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Game.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 선택 화면(설계 89 §6.2) - 설명문(즉시 전체 표시)·표시 인물(Placeholder 색 사각형 + 이름, 반전 없음)·선택지(제한시간 없음, 잠김은 흐림 + 잠김 문구).
    /// 인물·선택지 줄은 템플릿에서 처음 필요할 때만 만들고 이후 재사용한다. 루트 켜기·끄기는 패널(ViewRootPanel)이 한다.
    /// </summary>
    public class FieldEventChoiceView : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private const float LockedAlpha = 0.5f;

        [SerializeField] private TMP_Text descriptionText;
        [SerializeField] private GameObject personTemplate;
        [SerializeField] private Button optionTemplate;
        [SerializeField] private Button formationButton;

        private sealed class PersonRow { public GameObject Root; public Image Figure; public TMP_Text Name; }
        private sealed class OptionRow { public Button Button; public TMP_Text Label; public CanvasGroup Group; }

        private readonly List<PersonRow> people = new();
        private readonly List<OptionRow> options = new();

        public event Action FormationRequested;

        private void OnEnable() { if (formationButton != null) formationButton.onClick.AddListener(RaiseFormation); }
        private void OnDisable() { if (formationButton != null) formationButton.onClick.RemoveListener(RaiseFormation); }
        private void RaiseFormation() => FormationRequested?.Invoke();

        public void Show(EventChoiceContent content, Action<int> onChosen)
        {
            descriptionText.text = content.Description;

            for (var i = 0; i < content.People.Count; i++)
            {
                var row = GetPerson(i);
                row.Root.SetActive(true);
                var color = content.People[i].Color;
                color.a = 1f;
                row.Figure.color = color;
                row.Name.text = content.People[i].Name;
            }
            for (var i = content.People.Count; i < people.Count; i++) people[i].Root.SetActive(false);

            for (var i = 0; i < content.Options.Count; i++)
            {
                var row = GetOption(i);
                var option = content.Options[i];
                row.Button.gameObject.SetActive(true);
                row.Button.interactable = option.IsAvailable;
                row.Group.alpha = option.IsAvailable ? 1f : LockedAlpha;
                var text = $"{i + 1}. {option.Text}";
                row.Label.text = option.IsAvailable || option.LockText.Length == 0 ? text : $"{text} — {option.LockText}";
                row.Button.onClick.RemoveAllListeners();
                var index = option.Index;
                row.Button.onClick.AddListener(() => onChosen(index));
            }
            for (var i = content.Options.Count; i < options.Count; i++) options[i].Button.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        }

        private PersonRow GetPerson(int index)
        {
            while (people.Count <= index)
            {
                var go = Instantiate(personTemplate, personTemplate.transform.parent);
                people.Add(new PersonRow { Root = go, Figure = go.transform.Find("Figure").GetComponent<Image>(), Name = go.transform.Find("Name").GetComponent<TMP_Text>() });
            }
            return people[index];
        }

        private OptionRow GetOption(int index)
        {
            while (options.Count <= index)
            {
                var button = Instantiate(optionTemplate, optionTemplate.transform.parent);
                options.Add(new OptionRow { Button = button, Label = button.GetComponentInChildren<TMP_Text>(true), Group = button.GetComponent<CanvasGroup>() });
            }
            return options[index];
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 화면 묶음** — `FieldEventScreens.cs`

```csharp
using System;

namespace Game.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// IEventEncounterScreens의 Field 구현(설계 89 §6.3). 패널 열기·닫기는 전부 UIManager를 거친다 - 이벤트 화면·선택 화면·정비창·회수 적재가 같은 모달
    /// 묶음이라, 위에 다른 화면을 열면 숨기고 닫으면 되돌리는 규칙을 그대로 얻는다. 시작·종료에 같이 바뀌는 상단 버튼 비활성과 정비창 열람 모드도 여기서
    /// 묶는다. plain C# - FieldUIWiring이 Field 로드마다 새로 만들어 진행자에 연결한다(이벤트는 뷰 이벤트에 직접 붙여 따로 해제할 구독이 없다).
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class FieldEventScreens : IEventEncounterScreens
    {
        private readonly IUIManager uiManager;
        private readonly FieldEventDialogueView dialogue;
        private readonly FieldEventChoiceView choice;
        private readonly ViewRootPanel dialoguePanel;
        private readonly ViewRootPanel choicePanel;
        private readonly FieldCargoRecoveryPanel cargoRecovery;   // 선택적
        private readonly IFieldUIController fieldUI;
        private readonly IFormationViewOnlyMode formation;          // 선택적

        public FieldEventScreens(IUIManager uiManager, FieldEventDialogueView dialogue, FieldEventChoiceView choice, ViewRootPanel dialoguePanel, ViewRootPanel choicePanel,
            FieldCargoRecoveryPanel cargoRecovery, IFieldUIController fieldUI, IFormationViewOnlyMode formation)
        {
            this.uiManager = uiManager;
            this.dialogue = dialogue;
            this.choice = choice;
            this.dialoguePanel = dialoguePanel;
            this.choicePanel = choicePanel;
            this.cargoRecovery = cargoRecovery;
            this.fieldUI = fieldUI;
            this.formation = formation;
        }

        public bool IsAvailable => dialogue != null && choice != null;   // Unity 파괴 판정
        public IDialogueStageView DialogueStage => dialogue;

        public event Action Clicked { add => dialogue.Clicked += value; remove => dialogue.Clicked -= value; }

        public event Action FormationViewRequested
        {
            add { dialogue.FormationRequested += value; choice.FormationRequested += value; }
            remove { dialogue.FormationRequested -= value; choice.FormationRequested -= value; }
        }

        public event Action Reshown
        {
            add { dialoguePanel.Opened += value; choicePanel.Opened += value; }
            remove { dialoguePanel.Opened -= value; choicePanel.Opened -= value; }
        }

        public void ShowDialogue() => uiManager.Open(UIPanelIds.EventDialogue);
        public void HideDialogue() => uiManager.Close(UIPanelIds.EventDialogue);

        public void ShowChoice(EventChoiceContent content, Action<int> onChosen)
        {
            choice.Show(content, onChosen);
            uiManager.Open(UIPanelIds.EventChoice);
        }

        public void HideChoice() => uiManager.Close(UIPanelIds.EventChoice);

        public bool TryShowCargoRecovery(Action onDone)
        {
            if (cargoRecovery == null) return false;
            cargoRecovery.SetCompletion(() =>
            {
                uiManager.Close(UIPanelIds.CargoRecovery);
                onDone?.Invoke();
            });
            uiManager.Open(UIPanelIds.CargoRecovery);
            return true;
        }

        public void OpenFormationViewer() => uiManager.Open(UIPanelIds.Formation);

        public void SetEventControls(bool eventActive)
        {
            fieldUI?.SetTopLevelButtonsInteractable(!eventActive);
            formation?.SetViewOnly(eventActive);
        }
    }
}
```

> 주의: `Clicked` 등 이벤트 접근자에서 `dialogue`가 파괴됐으면 `+=`/`-=`가 파괴된 오브젝트의 필드에 접근한다 - C# 이벤트라 예외는 나지 않는다(관리 객체는 살아 있음). 진행자는 화면이 사라지면 `BindScreens(null)`로 해제한다.

- [ ] **Step 4: 조립** — `Editor/FieldEventUIBuilder.cs` (FieldDialogueUIBuilder와 같은 구조, `EditorUIBuilder` 공용 함수만 사용)

```csharp
using TMPro;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Game.Core.Editor
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Field 이벤트 대화·선택 화면(설계 89 §6)을 ContentRoot 아래 get-or-create로 조립한다. FieldUIInstaller가 부른다. 두 루트는 비활성으로 저장한다 - 패널
    /// (ViewRootPanel)이 연다. 반투명 배경이 입력을 막아 뒤의 이동 뷰 버튼이 눌리지 않는다. 그림·글자는 raycastTarget=false(대화 화면 클릭은 배경이 받는다).
    /// </summary>
    internal static class FieldEventUIBuilder
    {
        private static readonly Color DimColor = new(0f, 0f, 0f, 0.6f);
        private static readonly Color BubbleColor = new(0f, 0f, 0f, 0.8f);
        private static readonly Color BoxColor = new(0.1f, 0.1f, 0.12f, 0.9f);
        private static readonly Color OptionColor = new(0.95f, 0.92f, 0.85f, 1f);
        private static readonly Color ButtonColor = new(0.95f, 0.85f, 0.6f, 1f);
        private static readonly Vector2 FormationMin = new(0.02f, 0.02f);
        private static readonly Vector2 FormationMax = new(0.14f, 0.08f);

        public static void Build(Transform parent, Transform placeAfter)
        {
            var dialogue = BuildDialogue(parent);
            var choice = BuildChoice(parent);
            if (placeAfter != null)
            {
                EditorUIBuilder.PlaceSiblingAfter(dialogue, placeAfter);
                EditorUIBuilder.PlaceSiblingAfter(choice, dialogue);
            }
        }

        private static Transform BuildDialogue(Transform parent)
        {
            var root = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrCreateUIObject(parent, "EventDialogue");
            EditorUIBuilder.SetStretch(root.GetComponent<RectTransform>());
            EditorUIBuilder.EnsureMarker(root, FieldEventUIElementIds.DialogueRoot);

            var dim = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrCreateUIObject(root.transform, "Dim");
            EditorUIBuilder.SetStretch(dim.GetComponent<RectTransform>());
            EditorUIBuilder.EnsureImage(dim, DimColor).raycastTarget = true;
            var catcher = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrAddComponent<FieldDialogueClickCatcher>(dim);

            var body = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrCreateUIObject(root.transform, "Body");
            EditorUIBuilder.SetStretch(body.GetComponent<RectTransform>());

            // 스탠딩 왼쪽 x .04~.28, 오른쪽 x .72~.96, y .14~.86 - 말풍선보다 먼저 만들어 뒤에 그린다.
            BuildStanding(body.transform, "LeftStanding", new Vector2(0.04f, 0.14f), new Vector2(0.28f, 0.86f), flip: true, out var leftRoot, out var leftFigure, out var leftName);
            BuildStanding(body.transform, "RightStanding", new Vector2(0.72f, 0.14f), new Vector2(0.96f, 0.86f), flip: false, out var rightRoot, out var rightFigure, out var rightName);

            // 말풍선 x .30~.70, y .36~.62
            var bubble = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrCreateUIObject(body.transform, "Bubble");
            EditorUIBuilder.SetAnchors(bubble.GetComponent<RectTransform>(), new Vector2(0.30f, 0.36f), new Vector2(0.70f, 0.62f));
            EditorUIBuilder.EnsureImage(bubble, BubbleColor).raycastTarget = false;
            var speakerName = Text(bubble.transform, "SpeakerName", new Vector2(0.03f, 0.80f), new Vector2(0.97f, 0.97f), 24f, TextAlignmentOptions.Left, new Color(1f, 0.9f, 0.6f));
            var line = Text(bubble.transform, "Line", new Vector2(0.03f, 0.06f), new Vector2(0.97f, 0.80f), 26f, TextAlignmentOptions.TopLeft, Color.white);
            line.textWrappingMode = TextWrappingModes.Normal;
            var marker = Text(bubble.transform, "ContinueMarker", new Vector2(0.93f, 0.02f), new Vector2(0.99f, 0.16f), 20f, TextAlignmentOptions.Center, Color.white);
            marker.text = "▼";
            // 꼬리: 말풍선 양옆 가운데의 작은 사각형 - 말하는 쪽만 켠다.
            var leftTail = Tail(bubble.transform, "LeftTail", new Vector2(-0.03f, 0.40f), new Vector2(0f, 0.60f));
            var rightTail = Tail(bubble.transform, "RightTail", new Vector2(1f, 0.40f), new Vector2(1.03f, 0.60f));

            var formation = FormationButton(root.transform);

            var view = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrAddComponent<FieldEventDialogueView>(root);
            var so = new SerializedObject(view);
            so.FindProperty("body").objectReferenceValue = body;
            so.FindProperty("clickCatcher").objectReferenceValue = catcher;
            so.FindProperty("speakerNameText").objectReferenceValue = speakerName;
            so.FindProperty("lineText").objectReferenceValue = line;
            so.FindProperty("continueMarker").objectReferenceValue = marker.gameObject;
            so.FindProperty("leftTail").objectReferenceValue = leftTail;
            so.FindProperty("rightTail").objectReferenceValue = rightTail;
            so.FindProperty("leftRoot").objectReferenceValue = leftRoot;
            so.FindProperty("leftFigure").objectReferenceValue = leftFigure;
            so.FindProperty("leftName").objectReferenceValue = leftName;
            so.FindProperty("rightRoot").objectReferenceValue = rightRoot;
            so.FindProperty("rightFigure").objectReferenceValue = rightFigure;
            so.FindProperty("rightName").objectReferenceValue = rightName;
            so.FindProperty("formationButton").objectReferenceValue = formation;
            so.ApplyModifiedProperties();

            body.SetActive(false);
            root.SetActive(false);
            return root.transform;
        }

        private static Transform BuildChoice(Transform parent)
        {
            var root = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrCreateUIObject(parent, "EventChoice");
            EditorUIBuilder.SetStretch(root.GetComponent<RectTransform>());
            EditorUIBuilder.EnsureMarker(root, FieldEventUIElementIds.ChoiceRoot);

            var dim = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrCreateUIObject(root.transform, "Dim");
            EditorUIBuilder.SetStretch(dim.GetComponent<RectTransform>());
            EditorUIBuilder.EnsureImage(dim, DimColor).raycastTarget = true;

            // 설명문 x .15~.85, y .66~.90
            var box = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrCreateUIObject(root.transform, "DescriptionBox");
            EditorUIBuilder.SetAnchors(box.GetComponent<RectTransform>(), new Vector2(0.15f, 0.66f), new Vector2(0.85f, 0.90f));
            EditorUIBuilder.EnsureImage(box, BoxColor).raycastTarget = false;
            var description = Text(box.transform, "Description", new Vector2(0.03f, 0.08f), new Vector2(0.97f, 0.92f), 26f, TextAlignmentOptions.TopLeft, Color.white);
            description.textWrappingMode = TextWrappingModes.Normal;

            // 표시 인물 x .15~.85, y .40~.62 - 가로 정렬
            var peopleRow = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrCreateUIObject(root.transform, "People");
            EditorUIBuilder.SetAnchors(peopleRow.GetComponent<RectTransform>(), new Vector2(0.15f, 0.40f), new Vector2(0.85f, 0.62f));
            var peopleLayout = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrAddComponent<HorizontalLayoutGroup>(peopleRow);
            peopleLayout.spacing = 24f;
            peopleLayout.childAlignment = TextAnchor.MiddleCenter;
            peopleLayout.childControlWidth = true;
            peopleLayout.childControlHeight = true;
            peopleLayout.childForceExpandWidth = false;
            peopleLayout.childForceExpandHeight = true;
            var person = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrCreateUIObject(peopleRow.transform, "PersonTemplate");
            EditorUIBuilder.GetOrAddComponent<LayoutElement>(person).preferredWidth = 160f;
            var figure = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrCreateUIObject(person.transform, "Figure");
            EditorUIBuilder.SetAnchors(figure.GetComponent<RectTransform>(), new Vector2(0f, 0.18f), new Vector2(1f, 1f));
            EditorUIBuilder.EnsureImage(figure, Color.white).raycastTarget = false;
            Text(person.transform, "Name", new Vector2(0f, 0f), new Vector2(1f, 0.16f), 20f, TextAlignmentOptions.Center, Color.white);
            person.SetActive(false);

            // 선택지 x .20~.80, y .08~.36
            var optionsRoot = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrCreateUIObject(root.transform, "Options");
            EditorUIBuilder.SetAnchors(optionsRoot.GetComponent<RectTransform>(), new Vector2(0.20f, 0.08f), new Vector2(0.80f, 0.36f));
            var optionsLayout = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrAddComponent<VerticalLayoutGroup>(optionsRoot);
            optionsLayout.spacing = 6f;
            optionsLayout.childControlWidth = true;
            optionsLayout.childControlHeight = true;
            optionsLayout.childForceExpandWidth = true;
            optionsLayout.childForceExpandHeight = false;
            var option = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrCreateUIObject(optionsRoot.transform, "OptionTemplate");
            EditorUIBuilder.EnsureImage(option, OptionColor);
            var optionButton = EditorUIBuilder.EnsureButton(option);
            EditorUIBuilder.GetOrAddComponent<CanvasGroup>(option);
            EditorUIBuilder.GetOrAddComponent<LayoutElement>(option).preferredHeight = 44f;
            EditorUIBuilder.EnsureLabel(option.transform, string.Empty, autoSize: true, minFontSize: 16f, maxFontSize: 26f);
            option.SetActive(false);

            var formation = FormationButton(root.transform);

            var view = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrAddComponent<FieldEventChoiceView>(root);
            var so = new SerializedObject(view);
            so.FindProperty("descriptionText").objectReferenceValue = description;
            so.FindProperty("personTemplate").objectReferenceValue = person;
            so.FindProperty("optionTemplate").objectReferenceValue = optionButton;
            so.FindProperty("formationButton").objectReferenceValue = formation;
            so.ApplyModifiedProperties();

            root.SetActive(false);
            return root.transform;
        }

        private static Button FormationButton(Transform parent)
        {
            var go = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrCreateUIObject(parent, "FormationViewButton");
            EditorUIBuilder.SetAnchors(go.GetComponent<RectTransform>(), FormationMin, FormationMax);
            EditorUIBuilder.EnsureImage(go, ButtonColor);
            var button = EditorUIBuilder.EnsureButton(go);
            EditorUIBuilder.EnsureLabel(go.transform, "정비창 보기", autoSize: true, minFontSize: 16f, maxFontSize: 26f);
            go.transform.SetAsLastSibling();
            return button;
        }

        private static void BuildStanding(Transform parent, string name, Vector2 min, Vector2 max, bool flip, out GameObject root, out Image figure, out TMP_Text nameText)
        {
            root = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrCreateUIObject(parent, name);
            EditorUIBuilder.SetAnchors(root.GetComponent<RectTransform>(), min, max);
            var figureGo = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrCreateUIObject(root.transform, "Figure");
            var figureRect = figureGo.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
            EditorUIBuilder.SetAnchors(figureRect, new Vector2(0f, 0.08f), new Vector2(1f, 1f));
            // 일러1(왼쪽)만 그림을 좌우반전한다(설계 88 §5.2와 같음) - 이름은 별도 요소라 반전되지 않는다.
            figureRect.localScale = new Vector3(flip ? -1f : 1f, 1f, 1f);
            figure = EditorUIBuilder.EnsureImage(figureGo, Color.white);
            figure.raycastTarget = false;
            nameText = Text(root.transform, "Name", new Vector2(0f, 0f), new Vector2(1f, 0.07f), 22f, TextAlignmentOptions.Center, Color.white);
            root.SetActive(false);
        }

        private static GameObject Tail(Transform parent, string name, Vector2 min, Vector2 max)
        {
            var go = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrCreateUIObject(parent, name);
            EditorUIBuilder.SetAnchors(go.GetComponent<RectTransform>(), min, max);
            EditorUIBuilder.EnsureImage(go, BubbleColor).raycastTarget = false;
            go.SetActive(false);
            return go;
        }

        private static TextMeshProUGUI Text(Transform parent, string name, Vector2 min, Vector2 max, float size, TextAlignmentOptions alignment, Color color)
        {
            var go = EditorUIBuilder.GetOrCreateUIObject(parent, name);
            EditorUIBuilder.SetAnchors(go.GetComponent<RectTransform>(), min, max);
            var text = EditorUIBuilder.EnsureText(go);
            text.fontSize = size;
            text.enableAutoSizing = false;
            text.alignment = alignment;
            text.color = color;
            text.raycastTarget = false;
            return text;
        }
    }
}
```

> `Text`·`BuildStanding`이 FieldDialogueUIBuilder와 겹친다. 두 빌더가 같은 모양을 쓰므로 제작 시 `EditorUIBuilder`에 `CreateText(parent, name, min, max, size, alignment, color)`·`BuildStandingSlot(...)`로 옮기고 두 빌더가 그것을 부르게 한다(공용 조립은 EditorUIBuilder 규칙). 옮기면서 FieldDialogueUIBuilder의 결과(이름·좌표)가 바뀌지 않아야 한다 - Build Field Scene 재실행 후 대화 화면 회귀 확인.

`FieldUIInstaller.cs` — `BuildCargoRecoveryPanel(contentRoot, resultPopup);` 다음 줄:

```csharp
            FieldEventUIBuilder.Build(contentRoot, contentRoot.Find("CargoRecoveryPanel"));
```

`Wire`의 `BindTripDialogue(registrar, sceneUIRoot);` 다음 줄:

```csharp
            BindEventEncounter(registrar, sceneUIRoot, uiManager, panelRegistrar, fieldUIController, formationPanel.GetComponent<IFormationViewOnlyMode>());
```

```csharp
        // 이벤트 화면(설계 89 §6.3) - 진행자는 Bootstrap 상주라 Field를 로드할 때마다 이번 씬의 화면 묶음으로 바꿔 끼운다. 두 화면은 정비창과 같은 모달 묶음.
        private void BindEventEncounter(IDependencyResolver registrar, SceneUIRoot sceneUIRoot, IUIManager uiManager, IPanelRegistrar panelRegistrar, IFieldUIController fieldUIController, IFormationViewOnlyMode viewOnly)
        {
            if (!registrar.TryResolve<IEventEncounterViewBinder>(out var binder)) return;
            var ok = sceneUIRoot.TryGetElement(FieldEventUIElementIds.DialogueRoot, out FieldEventDialogueView dialogue)
                & sceneUIRoot.TryGetElement(FieldEventUIElementIds.ChoiceRoot, out FieldEventChoiceView choice);
            if (!ok)
            {
                Debug.LogWarning("이벤트 화면 요소가 없어 이벤트가 발생하지 않는다(Tools > Game > Build Field Scene).");
                binder.BindScreens(null);
                return;
            }

            var dialoguePanel = new ViewRootPanel(UIPanelIds.EventDialogue, dialogue.gameObject);
            var choicePanel = new ViewRootPanel(UIPanelIds.EventChoice, choice.gameObject);
            panelRegistrar.RegisterPopupPanel(dialoguePanel);
            panelRegistrar.RegisterPopupPanel(choicePanel);
            binder.BindScreens(new FieldEventScreens(uiManager, dialogue, choice, dialoguePanel, choicePanel, cargoRecoveryPanel, fieldUIController, viewOnly));
        }
```

